# Original-row dual and frozen finite discovery design

Written before any mathematical child is launched. Allocation: public
`640f5a8af9ca9dba`; model gpt-6-astra, effort ultra, one invocation. This is
an exact interface derivation and two exact calibrations, followed by a
separately labelled binary64 discovery design. No new fact is submitted.

## 1. Governing sources and scope

The current `PROBLEM.md` defines W=2(f_n-f_star)/(L||x0-xstar||^2).
Accepted `1252b8823bb5a290` fixes the interpolation orientation and the
unqueried-terminal square decomposition. Its existence theorem deletes
incoming terminal rows; it does not authorize deletion of optimum-source
rows in every optimal certificate. Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` gives the
full-class equality W_7(H3)=1/(1+2S3) for a fixed enclosing M>=1536.
Its scope does not include H4 or arbitrary depth. The public max4 task
`TASK_max4_astra_ultra_unsaturated_terminal_20261003.md` is analytical
context, not a premise or a numerical authorization. Its own numerical
allowance remains zero. The present six-job allocation is independent.

## 2. Every original row and the formal matrix

Normalize L=1 and xstar=gstar=fstar=0. The sampled labels are
I={0,...,n,*}; indices 0,...,n-1 are queried by the updates, while n is
the original unqueried terminal. The formal vectors are independent
coordinates (x0,g0,...,gn), of size d=n+2. Put

    p_i=e_0-sum_(t<i) h_t e_(t+1), u_i=e_(i+1)  (0<=i<=n),
    p_*=u_*=0, x_i=p_i^T z, g_i=u_i^T z.

For every ordered pair, including optimum, terminal, and diagonal, use

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0.
    A_ij=Sym(u_i (p_j-p_i)^T)+(u_i-u_j)(u_i-u_j)^T/2,
    Sym(B)=(B+B^T)/2.

Thus the gradient part is z^T A_ij z. Diagonal rows are identically zero.
The finite value-flow matrix has column (i,j) equal to e_i-e_j, omitting
the fixed optimum coordinate. In the full polynomial

    q||x0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i,j in I) lambda_ij Q_ij,

the coefficient of each f_i, 0<=i<=n, is
out_i-in_i-2*1_(i=n). Exact function cancellation therefore requires

    out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n), 0<=i<=n.

There is no independent optimum-value variable; summing the finite flows
gives out_*-in_*=-2. After cancellation the complete original matrix is

    M(q,lambda)=q e_0 e_0^T+sum_(i,j in I) lambda_ij A_ij.

All multipliers are nonnegative and M must be PSD on the entire formal
space. For an actual normalized trajectory, nonpositive Q rows give
0<=z^T M z<=q||x0||^2-2f_n. Hence exact feasibility is sufficient for
W_n(h)<=q. Numerical feasibility is not that exact assertion.

## 3. Declared restrictions and complete free-terminal interface

This batch fixes lambda_ii=0, lambda_(i,n)=0 for i!=n, and
lambda_(*,i)=0 for all i. The last restriction is an additional search
restriction, not a theorem about all duals. No other ordered prefix row
is removed. These fixed entries are represented by literal zero constants;
every remaining original multiplier is an independent nonnegative variable.

Terminal flow gives sum_(i<n)lambda_(n,i)+lambda_(n,*)=2. Define

    pi=lambda_(n,*)/2, alpha_i=lambda_(n,i)/2,
    pi>=0, alpha_i>=0, pi+sum_(i<n)alpha_i=1.

Writing mu for the prefix multipliers gives the full remaining flows

    sum_(j in {0,...,n-1,*})mu_ij
       -sum_(j<n)mu_ji=2alpha_i, i<n,
    sum_(i<n)mu_(i,*)=2(1-pi).

The diagonal mu entries and optimum-source entries are fixed zero. Let
y_i=x_i-g_i, delta_i=f_i-||g_i||^2/2, and

    w_i=pi+sum_(j<=i)alpha_j,
    r_i=h_i*w_i-alpha_i,
    V_alpha=x_n-sum_(i<n)alpha_i*y_i=pi*x0-sum_(i<n)r_i*g_i.

Direct expansion of the terminal rows gives

    2sum_(i<n)alpha_i Q_(n,i)+2pi Q_(n,*)
      =2f_n-2sum_(i<n)alpha_i delta_i
         +||g_n-V_alpha||^2-||V_alpha||^2.

Consequently M=||g_n-V_alpha||^2+R, where the complete prefix polynomial is

    R=q||x0||^2-||V_alpha||^2
        +sum_(i<n)alpha_i||g_i||^2
        +sum_(i,j in prefix)mu_ij (gradient part of Q_ij).

The value terms cancel by the displayed prefix flows. The entire prefix
matrix has blocks [[q-pi^2,b^T],[b,K]], with

    b_i=pi*r_i-mu_(i,*)/2,
    K=diag(alpha)-r*r^T+sum mu_ij (A_ij)_(gradient,gradient).

This is equivalent to full PSD because the free formal gn may be set
equal to V_alpha. In particular q>=pi^2 is necessary. If q>pi^2 the
exact Schur condition is K-b*b^T/(q-pi^2)>=0. If q=pi^2, PSD instead
requires b=0 and K>=0. Thus imposing pi=sqrt(q) forces
mu_(i,*)=2pi*r_i; the free-pi problem does not impose those equalities.
No sign of r is imposed separately. The numerical model uses the full
original matrix M and original flow, not this eliminated Schur formulation.

For the requested diagnostic let prefix_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, S=sum h_i,
D=1+2S, and B=sum alpha_i*(1+prefix_i). Then sum r_i=S-B=:V.
On a common-gradient formal prefix the complete R becomes the 2 by 2 form

    [[q-pi^2, pi*(V+1)-1],
     [pi*(V+1)-1, D-(V+1)^2]].

Indeed sum b=pi*V-(1-pi), and the weighted prefix flows give the gradient
coefficient sum_i alpha_i+sum mu_ij A_ij(g,g)=2B. This is a necessary
restriction of full PSD, not a sufficient test. B, r, and this small matrix
will be exported as diagnostics inside each timed child; no such scalar
screen replaces the full original eigenvalue checks.

## 4. Exact feasible calibration

For h=(1), x1=x0-g0 and q=1/3. Set lambda_10=lambda_(1,*)=
lambda_(0,*)=1; all other multipliers are zero. Node 0 has outgoing1 and
incoming1; node 1 has outgoing2 and incoming0. Thus pi=alpha_0=1/2.
The gradient parts are

    A_10: (||g1||^2+||g0||^2)/2,
    A_(1,*): -<g1,x0>+<g1,g0>+||g1||^2/2,
    A_(0,*): -<g0,x0>+||g0||^2/2.

Their sum with q||x0||^2 is

    ||g1-x0/2+g0/2||^2+(1/12)||x0-3g0||^2,

whose matrix in (x0,g0,g1) is

    [[1/3,-1/2,-1/2],[-1/2,1,1/2],[-1/2,1/2,1]].

It is PSD by its displayed squares. The first timed child compares the
specified lambda combination of the ORIGINAL constructed A rows against
the two outer-product squares and the literal displayed matrix, preserving
all differences. It then makes its single free-pi minimization solve using
the same construction as every later case. No explicit certificate is
substituted for the solver output and no warm start is supplied.

## 5. Exact infeasible calibration

For h=(3/2,9/4,3/2), use the legal function f(x)=x^2/2 with x0=1.
Its actual positions and gradients are (1,-1/2,5/8,-5/16), since

    (1-3/2)(1-9/4)(1-3/2)=(-1/2)(-5/4)(-1/2)=-5/16.

The normalized payoff is 2f3=25/256, and

    25/256-17/184=(575-544)/5888=31/5888>0.

Every original Q_ij of this unit-curvature quadratic is zero, including
all optimum and terminal rows. For any exact flow-cancelling multipliers,
evaluation of M on this formal tuple would therefore be -31/5888<0.
No original full-row PSD dual can exist, even before our extra restrictions.
This scalar counterexample is the exact ground truth. The second child
preserves its exact Fraction arithmetic, all original witness residuals,
and the single free-pi solver result. Solver status alone is not the proof.

## 6. Frozen production cases and numerical acceptance vocabulary

Use exactly M=1536, epsilon=1/(4M), beta=1-epsilon,
c=(1+sqrt(2))*(1+epsilon), a=beta/c and
H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Build only H3 and H4, retaining the
same enclosing parameters. Fix q=17/[16(1+2sum H_j)]. The four production
cases are H3 free, H3 saturated, H4 free, H4 saturated in that order.
Free means minimize pi at this fixed q; saturated means pi=sqrt(q)
with the constant feasibility objective zero. No other quantity is optimized.

One CLARABEL solve through CVXPY is allowed per job, with the exact options
in cases.json. Selection uses existing public runtime metadata; availability
is checked only inside charged children. No fallback, installation, retry,
conditioning change, PSD repair, eigenvalue clipping, or multiplier clipping
is allowed. IEEE754 binary64 approximates production sqrt(2) and q.

The feasible calibration passes only on solver status `optimal`, completed
exports, all finite original coefficients, max original flow defect<=1e-7,
min original multiplier>=-1e-7, min original matrix eigenvalue>=-1e-7,
max independent reconstruction difference<=1e-7, exact structural zeros,
and both explicit certificate matrix comparisons<=1e-12. The explicit
certificate flow and nonnegativity checks must also pass. The infeasible
calibration passes only on status `infeasible`, completed original-row
counterexample checks, and exact Fraction arithmetic matching the displayed
values. Any different status, exception, timeout, or incomplete output fails
that calibration and retires all unused credit before production. These
tolerances gate this discovery workflow only and never certify exact PSD.

Production diagnostics retain every original ordered multiplier (including
fixed zeros and any negative returned free coordinate), full original row
matrices, complete original matrix, independently reconstructed matrix,
all original finite/optimum flows, every equality defect, entire spectrum,
constraint duals, native solver data when exposed, and the requested alpha
and B diagnostics. An infeasible solver may return no original multiplier
vector; then every original entry is explicitly unavailable/null, while
native conic rays are retained separately and never called multipliers.

## 7. External timing and freeze

Before the first launch, DERIVATION.md, cases.json, math_child.py, broker.py,
TASK.md, BUDGET_INITIAL.json, and named source hashes are frozen in
FREEZE.json. No source/case/derivation edit is permitted afterward.
The broker is stdlib-only: it handles hashes, text, process groups, control
booleans, and ledger arithmetic. It never loads mathematical arrays or
evaluates numerical criteria. Each child receives a new process group,
with a timer outside it that sends SIGKILL to the entire group at15 seconds.
Every full15-second charge is persisted BEFORE Popen. Imports, model build,
one solve, all diagnostics and all mathematical export remain within the
child. No mathematical postprocessing runs after it exits.

Exactly six slots and90 accounted seconds exist. Actual external elapsed

# Closed handoff: terminal-mass finite discovery

STOP. Allocation640f5a8af9ca9dba is closed after the first calibration.
There is no remaining credit and no authorization to resume, repair the
frozen programs, relax the gate, rerun, or replace a case.

## Result to carry forward

The exact original-row dual interface and both exact calibration
derivations are in the immutable `DERIVATION.md`. One numerical child
was launched: h=(1), q=1/3, minimize pi. The explicit supplied
certificate matched the original row matrix exactly in binary64;
residual criteria passed. The solver returned `optimal_inaccurate`
(native `AlmostSolved`), whereas the frozen gate required `optimal`.
The calibration therefore failed and all five remaining slots were
retired. There is no numerical H3 or H4 result from this batch.

This is a workflow calibration failure, not evidence that the exact
h=(1),q=1/3 dual is infeasible. Its exact two-square certificate remains
the supplied ground truth. Likewise the second calibration's scalar
payoff25/256>17/184 was derived before execution but was not numerically
tested. Neither numerical status nor this packet is a new Danus fact.

## Complete interface needed for interpretation

L=1 and xstar=gstar=fstar=0; formal vectors are (x0,g0,...,gn), with
x_i=x0-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t. Every original ordered interpolation row is
Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0. The certificate is
q||x0||^2-2f_n+sum lambda_ij Q_ij; function flow requires
out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n). Its entire remaining quadratic must be PSD.

The declared restrictions are diagonal multipliers zero, all incoming
terminal multipliers zero, and all optimum-source multipliers zero.
Every other original multiplier is nonnegative. The last restriction
is additional and does not characterize every possible dual.
Set pi=lambda_(n,*)/2, alpha_i=lambda_(n,i)/2; terminal flow gives
pi+sum alpha_i=1. With r_i=h_i*(pi+sum_(j<=i)alpha_j)-alpha_i, the
terminal square is ||gn-pi*x0+sum r_i*g_i||^2. The complete prefix
remainder block is [[q-pi^2,b^T],[b,K]], b_i=pi*r_i-mu_(i,*)/2.
Saturation forces b=0. Free pi retains the complete PSD block and its
mixed terms. The actual solver construction used the uneliminated
original matrix and original flow, not a scalar diagnostic relaxation.

selection scope. Accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4 gives only the full-class H3
equality at fixed enclosing M>=1536. H4/all-depth feasibility and the
original asymptotic Goal remain unresolved by this allocation.

The batch is CLOSED_CALIBRATION_FAILURE. Its first and only mathematical
job completed its exports, but returned CLARABEL `AlmostSolved` /
CVXPY `optimal_inaccurate`. The preregistered feasible-calibration gate
required `optimal`; consequently no second calibration or production

comparison. The original Goal remains OPEN.

allocation is separate from the four Danus analytical workers and from

change to another allocation was performed.

`DERIVATION.md` gives the original interpolation rows, complete original
formal-vector matrix, exact finite-node function flow, declared zero-row
restrictions, terminal probabilities and the entire prefix Schur block.

# Original-row dual with a strictly feasible one-step calibration

Written before any mathematical child is launched. Allocation: public
`b004c18926875e6b`; model gpt-6-astra, effort ultra, one invocation. This is
an exact interface derivation and two exact calibrations, followed by a
separately labelled binary64 discovery design. No new fact is submitted.

The historical source directory is `../terminal_mass_dual_discovery_20261003`.
Its closed allocation640f5a8af9ca9dba consumed1 job/15 accounted seconds
and retired5 jobs/75 seconds after native AlmostSolved / optimal_inaccurate.
It is immutable and contributes no credit here. Its original-row model,
negative calibration, production cases, solver and tolerances are retained.
The positive calibration changes from h=(1),q=1/3 to h=(1),q=3/8 and
adds the exact minimizer check below. The external deadline/precharge
changes from15 to20 seconds in this NEW grant. `SOURCE_DIFF.patch`
records the exact source, case and derivation differences. No inference
is made that the old boundary degeneracy alone caused its solver status.

## 1. Governing sources and scope

The current `PROBLEM.md` defines W=2(f_n-f_star)/(L||x0-xstar||^2).
Accepted `1252b8823bb5a290` fixes the interpolation orientation and the
unqueried-terminal square decomposition. Its existence theorem deletes
incoming terminal rows; it does not authorize deletion of optimum-source
rows in every optimal certificate. Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` gives the
full-class equality W_7(H3)=1/(1+2S3) for a fixed enclosing M>=1536.
Its scope does not include H4 or arbitrary depth. The public max4 task
`TASK_max4_astra_ultra_unsaturated_terminal_20261003.md` is analytical
context, not a premise or a numerical authorization. Its own numerical
allowance remains zero. The present six-job allocation is independent.

## 2. Every original row and the formal matrix

Normalize L=1 and xstar=gstar=fstar=0. The sampled labels are
I={0,...,n,*}; indices 0,...,n-1 are queried by the updates, while n is
the original unqueried terminal. The formal vectors are independent
coordinates (x0,g0,...,gn), of size d=n+2. Put

    p_i=e_0-sum_(t<i) h_t e_(t+1), u_i=e_(i+1)  (0<=i<=n),
    p_*=u_*=0, x_i=p_i^T z, g_i=u_i^T z.

For every ordered pair, including optimum, terminal, and diagonal, use

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2 <=0.
    A_ij=Sym(u_i (p_j-p_i)^T)+(u_i-u_j)(u_i-u_j)^T/2,
    Sym(B)=(B+B^T)/2.

Thus the gradient part is z^T A_ij z. Diagonal rows are identically zero.
The finite value-flow matrix has column (i,j) equal to e_i-e_j, omitting
the fixed optimum coordinate. In the full polynomial

    q||x0||^2-2f_n+sum_(i,j in I) lambda_ij Q_ij,

the coefficient of each f_i, 0<=i<=n, is
out_i-in_i-2*1_(i=n). Exact function cancellation therefore requires

    out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n), 0<=i<=n.

There is no independent optimum-value variable; summing the finite flows
gives out_*-in_*=-2. After cancellation the complete original matrix is

    M(q,lambda)=q e_0 e_0^T+sum_(i,j in I) lambda_ij A_ij.

All multipliers are nonnegative and M must be PSD on the entire formal
space. For an actual normalized trajectory, nonpositive Q rows give
0<=z^T M z<=q||x0||^2-2f_n. Hence exact feasibility is sufficient for
W_n(h)<=q. Numerical feasibility is not that exact assertion.

## 3. Declared restrictions and complete free-terminal interface

This batch fixes lambda_ii=0, lambda_(i,n)=0 for i!=n, and
lambda_(*,i)=0 for all i. The last restriction is an additional search
restriction, not a theorem about all duals. No other ordered prefix row
is removed. These fixed entries are represented by literal zero constants;
every remaining original multiplier is an independent nonnegative variable.

Terminal flow gives sum_(i<n)lambda_(n,i)+lambda_(n,*)=2. Define

    pi=lambda_(n,*)/2, alpha_i=lambda_(n,i)/2,
    pi>=0, alpha_i>=0, pi+sum_(i<n)alpha_i=1.

Writing mu for the prefix multipliers gives the full remaining flows

    sum_(j in {0,...,n-1,*})mu_ij
       -sum_(j<n)mu_ji=2alpha_i, i<n,
    sum_(i<n)mu_(i,*)=2(1-pi).

The diagonal mu entries and optimum-source entries are fixed zero. Let
y_i=x_i-g_i, delta_i=f_i-||g_i||^2/2, and

    w_i=pi+sum_(j<=i)alpha_j,
    r_i=h_i*w_i-alpha_i,
    V_alpha=x_n-sum_(i<n)alpha_i*y_i=pi*x0-sum_(i<n)r_i*g_i.

Direct expansion of the terminal rows gives

    2sum_(i<n)alpha_i Q_(n,i)+2pi Q_(n,*)
      =2f_n-2sum_(i<n)alpha_i delta_i
         +||g_n-V_alpha||^2-||V_alpha||^2.

Consequently M=||g_n-V_alpha||^2+R, where the complete prefix polynomial is

    R=q||x0||^2-||V_alpha||^2
        +sum_(i<n)alpha_i||g_i||^2
        +sum_(i,j in prefix)mu_ij (gradient part of Q_ij).

The value terms cancel by the displayed prefix flows. The entire prefix
matrix has blocks [[q-pi^2,b^T],[b,K]], with

    b_i=pi*r_i-mu_(i,*)/2,
    K=diag(alpha)-r*r^T+sum mu_ij (A_ij)_(gradient,gradient).

This is equivalent to full PSD because the free formal gn may be set
equal to V_alpha. In particular q>=pi^2 is necessary. If q>pi^2 the
exact Schur condition is K-b*b^T/(q-pi^2)>=0. If q=pi^2, PSD instead
requires b=0 and K>=0. Thus imposing pi=sqrt(q) forces
mu_(i,*)=2pi*r_i; the free-pi problem does not impose those equalities.
No sign of r is imposed separately. The numerical model uses the full
original matrix M and original flow, not this eliminated Schur formulation.

For the requested diagnostic let prefix_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, S=sum h_i,
D=1+2S, and B=sum alpha_i*(1+prefix_i). Then sum r_i=S-B=:V.
On a common-gradient formal prefix the complete R becomes the 2 by 2 form

    [[q-pi^2, pi*(V+1)-1],
     [pi*(V+1)-1, D-(V+1)^2]].

Indeed sum b=pi*V-(1-pi), and the weighted prefix flows give the gradient
coefficient sum_i alpha_i+sum mu_ij A_ij(g,g)=2B. This is a necessary
restriction of full PSD, not a sufficient test. B, r, and this small matrix
will be exported as diagnostics inside each timed child; no such scalar
screen replaces the full original eigenvalue checks.

## 4. New exact feasible calibration and independent elimination

For h=(1), x1=x0-g0 and q=3/8, the allowed original rows are exactly
Q_10,Q_(1,*),Q_(0,*). All other multipliers are fixed zero by the declared
restrictions. Direct original-row expansion, before any elimination, gives

    A_10: (||g1||^2+||g0||^2)/2,
    A_(1,*): -<g1,x0>+<g1,g0>+||g1||^2/2,
    A_(0,*): -<g0,x0>+||g0||^2/2.

The flow at0 is lambda_(0,*)-lambda_10=0 and at1 is
lambda_10+lambda_(1,*)=2. Consequently every legal multiplier vector is

    lambda_(1,*)=2pi,
    lambda_10=lambda_(0,*)=2(1-pi),  0<=pi<=1.

The optimum flow is then exactly-2. In the original order (x0,g0,g1)
the full matrix derived from these THREE original rows is

    M(q,pi)=[[q, -(1-pi), -pi],
             [-(1-pi), 2(1-pi), pi],
             [-pi, pi, 1]].

Completing only the actual terminal square gives

    M(q,pi)=||g1-pi*x0+pi*g0||^2+R(q,pi),
    R(q,pi)=[[q-pi^2, pi^2+pi-1],
             [pi^2+pi-1, 2-2pi-pi^2]].

Here the first equality denotes equality of quadratic forms. The change
from g1 to g1-pi*x0+pi*g0 is invertible and has determinant1. Thus
M is PSD exactly when R is PSD, and their determinants agree. Expanding
the actual two-by-two determinant independently yields

    det M=det R
      =(q-pi^2)(2-2pi-pi^2)-(pi^2+pi-1)^2
      =2q-1+2(1-q)pi-(1+q)pi^2.

At q=3/8 this becomes

    (-2+10pi-11pi^2)/8,
    pi_-=(5-sqrt(3))/11, pi_+=(5+sqrt(3))/11.

It is nonnegative exactly on [pi_-,pi_+]. There is no lost sign or
principal-minor condition. Write s=sqrt(3), so1<s<2. The endpoints
satisfy0<pi_-<=pi_+<1 and pi_->1/4, since4s<9. Throughout the interval,

    q-pi^2 >= q-pi_+^2=(139-80s)/968>0,
    2-2pi-pi^2 >= (104-32s)/121>0.

The first strict sign follows from139^2-(80s)^2=121>0 and139,80s>0;
the second follows from s<2. These verify both diagonal entries of R.
For completeness the original one-by-one principal minors are
q,2(1-pi),1, all positive; its two-by-two principal minors are

    (1-pi)(2q-1+pi)=(1-pi)(pi-1/4),
    q-pi^2,
    2-2pi-pi^2,

all positive on this interval. Its full determinant is nonnegative.
Conversely, PSD requires this full determinant nonnegative, giving exactly
the same interval; multiplier nonnegativity imposes no additional cut.
Therefore the exact free-pi optimum in this restricted original-row model
is pi_-=(5-sqrt(3))/11. The endpoint singularity of an optimized matrix
does not affect the existence of the strictly feasible point next.

At pi=1/2, set lambda_10=lambda_(1,*)=lambda_(0,*)=1. The finite flows
are(0,2), the optimum flow is-2, and all permitted multipliers are positive.
The original quadratic is exactly

    ||g1-x0/2+g0/2||^2+(1/12)||x0-3g0||^2+(1/24)||x0||^2,

with literal matrix

    [[3/8,-1/2,-1/2],[-1/2,1,1/2],[-1/2,1/2,1]].

Its square coefficient vectors(-1/2,1/2,1),(1,-3,0),(1,0,0) are
independent: the matrix with those rows has determinant3. All square
weights are strictly positive. Its principal minors are3/8,1,1;
1/8,1/8,3/4; and det M=1/32. This verifies strict positive definiteness
on the entire original three-vector formal space, as well as exact flow
and signs. No numeric computation supplies this derivation.

The first timed child compares the specified lambda combination of the
ORIGINAL constructed A rows against both the three-square outer products
and the literal matrix, preserving all differences. It then makes ONE
free-pi minimization solve through the unchanged full production model.
The explicit certificate is not substituted for solver output; warm_start
is false. The original optimal-status and residual gate remains in force,
and returned pi must additionally lie within1e-7 of pi_-.

## 5. Exact infeasible calibration

For h=(3/2,9/4,3/2), use the legal function f(x)=x^2/2 with x0=1.
Its actual positions and gradients are (1,-1/2,5/8,-5/16), since

    (1-3/2)(1-9/4)(1-3/2)=(-1/2)(-5/4)(-1/2)=-5/16.

The normalized payoff is 2f3=25/256, and

    25/256-17/184=(575-544)/5888=31/5888>0.

Every original Q_ij of this unit-curvature quadratic is zero, including
all optimum and terminal rows. For any exact flow-cancelling multipliers,
evaluation of M on this formal tuple would therefore be -31/5888<0.
No original full-row PSD dual can exist, even before our extra restrictions.
This scalar counterexample is the exact ground truth. The second child
preserves its exact Fraction arithmetic, all original witness residuals,
and the single free-pi solver result. Solver status alone is not the proof.

## 6. Frozen production cases and numerical acceptance vocabulary

Use exactly M=1536, epsilon=1/(4M), beta=1-epsilon,
c=(1+sqrt(2))*(1+epsilon), a=beta/c and
H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Build only H3 and H4, retaining the
same enclosing parameters. Fix q=17/[16(1+2sum H_j)]. The four production
cases are H3 free, H3 saturated, H4 free, H4 saturated in that order.
Free means minimize pi at this fixed q; saturated means pi=sqrt(q)
with the constant feasibility objective zero. No other quantity is optimized.

One CLARABEL solve through CVXPY is allowed per job, with the exact options
in cases.json. Selection uses existing public runtime metadata; availability
is checked only inside charged children. No fallback, installation, retry,
conditioning change, PSD repair, eigenvalue clipping, or multiplier clipping
is allowed. IEEE754 binary64 approximates production sqrt(2) and q.

The feasible calibration passes only on solver status `optimal`, completed
exports, all finite original coefficients, max original flow defect<=1e-7,
min original multiplier>=-1e-7, min original matrix eigenvalue>=-1e-7,
max independent reconstruction difference<=1e-7, exact structural zeros,
and both explicit certificate matrix comparisons<=1e-12. The explicit
certificate flow and nonnegativity checks must also pass. The feasible
calibration's returned pi must be within1e-7 of(5-sqrt(3))/11.
The infeasible calibration passes only on status `infeasible`, completed original-row
counterexample checks, and exact Fraction arithmetic matching the displayed
values. Any different status, exception, timeout, or incomplete output fails
that calibration and retires all unused credit before production. These
tolerances gate this discovery workflow only and never certify exact PSD.

Production diagnostics retain every original ordered multiplier (including
fixed zeros and any negative returned free coordinate), full original row
matrices, complete original matrix, independently reconstructed matrix,
all original finite/optimum flows, every equality defect, entire spectrum,
constraint duals, native solver data when exposed, and the requested alpha
and B diagnostics. An infeasible solver may return no original multiplier
vector; then every original entry is explicitly unavailable/null, while
native conic rays are retained separately and never called multipliers.

## 7. External timing and freeze

Before the first launch, DERIVATION.md, cases.json, math_child.py, broker.py,
TASK.md, BUDGET_INITIAL.json, SOURCE_DIFF.patch, and named source hashes are frozen in
FREEZE.json. No source/case/derivation edit is permitted afterward.
The broker is stdlib-only: it handles hashes, text, process groups, control
booleans, and ledger arithmetic. It never loads mathematical arrays or
evaluates numerical criteria. Each child receives a new process group,
with a timer outside it that sends SIGKILL to the entire group at20 seconds.
Every full20-second charge is persisted BEFORE Popen. Imports, model build,
one solve, all diagnostics and all mathematical export remain within the
child. No mathematical postprocessing runs after it exits.

Exactly six NEW slots and120 accounted seconds exist. Actual external elapsed
time is recorded separately and cannot replenish credit. Failure consumes
its full charge. No replacement/renewal or formal/consult/subagent/lifecycle/
truth-store/outward action is licensed. All writes remain in this new
directory. The original Goal remains OPEN.

# Terminal-mass interior calibration: completed finite discovery

Allocation `b004c18926875e6b` is CLOSED_EXHAUSTED. Both prescribed
calibrations passed their frozen status and residual gates, and all four
prescribed H3/H4 production jobs completed. At the fixed production q,
both free-pi and saturated-pi models returned CLARABEL Solved / CVXPY
optimal with the saved original-residual checks true. This finite
comparison did not produce a numerical free-versus-saturated infeasibility
separation. It establishes no exact production certificate, H4 U law,
all-depth assertion, or resolution of the original minimax Goal.

This new allocation used exactly6 jobs/6 solves and120 ACCOUNTED seconds;
actual external child time was46.10697183996672 seconds. All six children
exited0 before their separate20-second process-group deadlines. Remaining
credit is0 jobs/0 seconds, with no automatic renewal. The historical
allocation640f5a8af9ca9dba stays closed and unchanged.

## 1. Authorization, frozen changes and evidence boundary

This is one direct Codex invocation using gpt-6-astra/ultra, independently
allocated from the four Danus analytical workers. TASK.md and the gateway
receipt identify the scope. DERIVATION.md, cases, both programs, the exact
readable SOURCE_DIFF.patch, initial budget and old-batch hash inventory
were frozen at2026-10-03T05:55:39.6155592Z. The first precharge occurred
later, at2026-10-03T05:56:03.214550+00:00. No frozen source was changed
afterward.

Only the positive calibration's q, its explicit three-square/literal
comparison, and the exact optimum-pi gate changed mathematically from the
historical code. The deadline and precharge changed from15 to20 seconds
under this new grant. Original interpolation rows, restrictions, negative
calibration, production schedules, fixed production q, solver and solver
options were retained exactly. No warm start, retry, repair, alternate
solver, fallback, conditioning change or installation was used.

The historical first job had returned optimal_inaccurate and stopped its
own batch. Its unused5 jobs/75 seconds were retired, and supplied no credit
here. Its failure does not demonstrate infeasibility of its exact q=1/3
certificate. This new calibration does not establish that boundary
degeneracy was the sole cause of the historical solver status.

The exact algebra below was completed in the frozen DERIVATION before
execution. Every subsequent numerical number in this report is copied
from an export produced inside a timed child. After budget closure only
existing text reads/transcription, report writing, hashes and accounting
were performed. No new numerical or symbolic diagnostic was executed.

## 2. Complete original-row interface

Normalize L=1 and xstar=gstar=fstar=0. For physical steps h0,...,h_(n-1),
use independent formal vectors (x0,g0,...,gn), including the ORIGINAL
unqueried terminal gn, with x_i=x0-sum_(t<i)h_t*g_t. The sample labels
are(0,...,n,*). For every ordered pair, including diagonal, optimum and
terminal pairs, define

    Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0.

If p_i and u_i are the position and gradient coefficient vectors, the
original row matrix is

    A_ij=Sym(u_i(p_j-p_i)^T)+(u_i-u_j)(u_i-u_j)^T/2.

The certificate polynomial is q||x0||^2-2f_n+sum lambda_ij Q_ij.
All finite-node value coefficients cancel exactly when

    out_i-in_i=2*1_(i=n), i=0,...,n.

The full optimum flow is then-2. The complete original quadratic matrix
is M=q e0e0^T+sum lambda_ij A_ij, and exact feasibility would require
lambda>=0 and M PSD on all n+2 original formal coordinates. The solver
imposed this uneliminated matrix and the original finite-node flow map.

The batch sets diagonal multipliers, all incoming-terminal multipliers,
and all optimum-source multipliers to literal zero. Every other ordered
prefix/terminal-outgoing row is retained with an independent nonnegative
coordinate. The optimum-source restriction is additional; it is not
asserted to characterize every optimal certificate.

Define pi=lambda_(n,*)/2, alpha_i=lambda_(n,i)/2 for i<n. Exact terminal
flow gives pi+sum alpha_i=1. With

    r_i=h_i*(pi+sum_(j<=i)alpha_j)-alpha_i,
    B=sum_(i<n)alpha_i*(1+sum_(t<i)h_t),

the terminal square is ||gn-pi*x0+sum r_i*g_i||^2. The complete prefix
remainder is [[q-pi^2,b^T],[b,K]], with
b_i=pi*r_i-mu_(i,*)/2 and mu the original prefix multipliers. At exact
saturation pi=sqrt(q), PSD requires b=0. For free pi, the entire prefix
matrix, including its mixed terms, must be PSD. Neither this Schur
description nor the exported common-gradient two-by-two diagnostic
replaced the original full matrix model or its eigenvalue reconstruction.

The earlier accepted1252b8823bb5a290 supplies the interpolation orientation
and the no-incoming-terminal selection scope; it does not remove all
optimum-source rows from every dual. Accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4 supplies the
scoped full-class seven-step H3 equality at fixed enclosing M>=1536.
Its assertion is not an H4/all-depth premise. No new fact was submitted.

## 3. Positive calibration: exact derivation and returned output

For h=(1),q=3/8, the only allowed multipliers satisfy

    lambda_(1,*)=2pi,
    lambda_10=lambda_(0,*)=2(1-pi), 0<=pi<=1.

Expanding those original rows gives

    M(q,pi)=[[q,-(1-pi),-pi],
             [-(1-pi),2(1-pi),pi],[-pi,pi,1]],
    R(q,pi)=[[q-pi^2,pi^2+pi-1],
             [pi^2+pi-1,2-2pi-pi^2]],
    det M=det R=2q-1+2(1-q)pi-(1+q)pi^2.

At q=3/8 the determinant is(-2+10pi-11pi^2)/8. The frozen derivation
checks all one-, two- and three-dimensional principal minors and every
multiplier sign. Its exact feasible interval is

    [(5-sqrt(3))/11,(5+sqrt(3))/11],

and the exact minimum is its lower endpoint. At pi=1/2, the three
nonzero multipliers are1 and the original quadratic is exactly

    ||g1-x0/2+g0/2||^2+(1/12)||x0-3g0||^2+(1/24)||x0||^2.

The three coefficient vectors are independent; their row determinant is3.
The literal matrix has diagonal minors3/8,1,1, two-by-two principal
minors1/8,1/8,3/4, and full determinant1/32. It is strictly positive
definite with exact flow(0,2,-2). The charged child compared this explicit
certificate with both its three-square matrix and the literal matrix;
every saved difference and flow defect was0.0. Its floating-point
spectrum was(0.03394798157493978,0.5,1.8410520184250592).

The single free-pi solve used the same construction as production:

## 4. Negative calibration

For h=(3/2,9/4,3/2),q=17/184, the prescribed exact unit quadratic
f(x)=x^2/2 with x0=1 has positions/gradients
(1,-1/2,5/8,-5/16). Its normalized payoff is25/256 and payoff-q=31/5888>0.
Every original Q_ij is exactly zero on this witness. The frozen exact
derivation therefore excludes a PSD original-row certificate at this q,
even without the batch's additional restrictions.

The charged child reproduced those Fraction values, saved all original
exact and floating-point witness residuals, and returned native
PrimalInfeasible / CVXPY infeasible after its one solve. The exact witness
check and calibration gate were both true. No original multiplier vector
was returned: all such entries, the matrix, flows and spectrum are
explicitly unavailable, not replaced by a conic ray. Native conic outputs
are retained separately in raw.json. The exact scalar witness supplies
the calibration ground truth; the numerical status does not supply a
new proof.

## 5. Frozen production data

The enclosing parameter remained M=1536 throughout. The exact design was
epsilon=1/(4M), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt(2))*(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
H0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), q=17/[16(1+2sum H_j)]. Only the
actual H3 and H4 words were built. They were not reset at subtrees.

The child-exported binary64 parameters were epsilon0.00016276041666666666,
beta0.9998372395833334, c2.4146065007784294, a0.41407874917134624.
The complete seven-step H3 word was

    [1.4140787491713462,1.9998372395833335,1.4140787491713462,
     3.4142134984182766,
     1.4140787491713462,1.9998372395833335,1.4140787491713462].

The complete fifteen-step H4 word was

Saved S values were13.07020297427033 for H3 and32.969781556088456 for H4.
The prescribed order was H3 free, H3 saturated, H4 free, H4 saturated.
Free mode minimizes pi at this fixed q. Saturated mode fixes pi=sqrt(q)
and has constant feasibility objective0. No q was optimized or changed.

## 8. Artifacts, scope and stop

Each of the six numbered job directories contains model.json, raw.json,
summary.json, gate.json, stdout.txt, stderr.txt, reservation.json and
receipt.json. model.json saves every original ordered-row matrix, all
position/gradient coordinates, allowed-pair order, fixed-zero mask and
original flow map. raw.json saves every returned original multiplier,
both full original matrices, original finite/optimum flows, all signed
defects and spectra, alpha, r, B, remainder diagnostics, constraint duals
and native conic data. Missing negative-calibration multipliers are marked
null with an explicit reason. No returned array was repaired.

FREEZE.json binds the immutable preregistration. OLD_BATCH_HASHES.json
records the untouched historical packet. FINAL_AUDIT.json records final
readback against those hashes and per-job receipts; MANIFEST.json binds
the completed packet. Hashes certify byte identity, not mathematical
truth. HANDOFF.md contains a self-contained continuation boundary.

Formal attempts/fact_submit, consults, subagents, worker/service control,
truth-store writes, outward publication, finalization, revocation,
shutdown and Goal completion were all ZERO. The four analytical workers
and their independent numerical/formal0 instructions were not changed.
The original minimax Goal remains OPEN. This grant is closed: STOP.

# Fresh terminal-mass discovery with a strictly feasible calibration point

ONE new native invocation, gpt-6-astra/ultra, consultOFF, formal0, original Goal OPEN. This is a new six-job/120-ACCOUNTED-second allocation, not a retry under the closed640f5a8af9ca9dba grant. Read OPERATOR.md and the FULL old DERIVATION/REPORT/HANDOFF in research/terminal_mass_dual_discovery_20261003. Its first calibration returned AlmostSolved/optimal_inaccurate and failed its frozen optimal gate even though the explicit matrix and residual checks passed. It consumed1job/15seconds and retired5jobs/75seconds; every old source, artifact and ledger must remain unchanged.

The old h=(1),q=1/3 feasible set is at the exact Huber boundary. Replace ONLY the positive calibration by h=(1),q=3/8, keeping the same full-original-row construction, restrictions, solver, solver tolerances, status gate, negative calibration and four H3/H4 production cases. This is not permission to accept optimal_inaccurate, repair numerical output or change production q. No claim is made that boundary degeneracy was the sole cause of the old native status.

Derive before execution the following new exact calibration ground truth. At the supplied lambda_10=lambda_1,*=lambda_0,*=1, the original matrix is
 ||g1-x0/2+g0/2||^2+(1/12)||x0-3g0||^2+(1/24)||x0||^2.
Its three square vectors are independent, hence this is strictly positive definite. The original flow and multiplier signs are exact. For this one-step free-pi model, elimination yields the exact feasible interval
 (5-sqrt3)/11 <= pi <= (5+sqrt3)/11,
so the minimized pi equals(5-sqrt3)/11. Independently verify the determinant and all principal/sign conditions; if the proposed exact interval is wrong, report and STOP before mathematics rather than freeze a false calibration. The full two-vector determinant is2q-1+2(1-q)pi-(1+q)pi^2. This formula is a lead to derive, not a substitute for original-matrix construction.

The new calibration checks the original explicit certificate against BOTH its three-square matrix and literal matrix with top-left3/8; it then makes ONE free-pi solve through the SAME production code. Keep the old optimal-status and raw residual requirements, and additionally require returned pi within1e-7 of the exact new target. Do not supply a warm start. The second calibration stays h=(3/2,9/4,3/2),q=17/184 with exact legal unit-quadratic payoff25/256 and expected infeasible status.

Jobs3-6 are UNCHANGED: fixed enclosing M=1536, actual H3/H4, q=17/[16(1+2sumH_j)], in order H3free,H3pi=sqrt(q),H4free,H4pi=sqrt(q). No other schedule, q, incoming-optimum assumption or original row is changed. Include the original unqueried terminal and all original row arrays exactly as in the prior full interface. No post hoc production-case selection.

You may COPY the old public code, cases and derivation as new starting files, and edit ONLY in this new directory BEFORE freezing. Required code changes are the positive calibration's q, literal/square comparison and new pi-target check; administrative changes are new paths/ids and external deadline20seconds. Record a readable exact diff. Retain CLARABEL and the old solver options including solver time_limit10seconds and tol1e-9. No alternative solver, fallback or hidden conditioning change. A new DERIVATION must explain the actual new calibration and identify historical source changes rather than retaining misleading q=1/3 calibration claims.

Exactly SIX prescribed jobs, each with an EXTERNAL process-group deadline20seconds, full20seconds precharged before launch, total120 ACCOUNTED seconds. Actual external elapsed recorded separately. Imports, model construction, ONE solve, complete original matrix/flow/eigen/residual reconstruction and all mathematical exports are inside the timed child. Freeze task/code/cases/derivation/budget/source hashes before first mathematical run. No post-freeze code change. On either calibration failure STOP before production and retire all unused slots. Failures,timeouts,malformed output or interruptions consume their full job/20seconds. No replacement,retry,extra job or automatic renewal. Old elapsed or retired credit is never reused.

Use only the existing public launcher research/run_shared_math_python.sh and existing runtime. No installation. Broker uses stdlib and control data only, no mathematical arrays. After closure only existing text reads/transcription, report writing and hashes: no extra mathematical extraction. Save ALL original multipliers, rows, matrices, flows, spectra and signed defects exactly as returned; no clipping, rationalization or hidden repair. Numerical feasibility/infeasibility is discovery evidence only and cannot be promoted to a theorem, H4 U law or all-depth bound.

Write full DERIVATION.md,REPORT.md,HANDOFF.md, raw outputs, precharge/exit/deadline receipts and final hash/budget readback exclusively under research/terminal_mass_interior_discovery_20261003. ZERO formal calls/fact_submit, consults, subagents, worker/service control, truth-store edits, outward publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown or Goal completion. All four Danus analytical workers retain their own numerical/formal0 tasks. Return and STOP at this new grant's boundary.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001477 -->
# Frozen outward-tree Huber pulse, max2 round58

Status: one selected analytical mechanism, not a verified result.
Authority: TASK.md and master_guidance c2181daa6b96056c.
Shared plan: 05c8afbb7b4b4d44. Mathematical repairs: zero.

For every integer m>=2 fix tau=1/4, lambda=1+sqrt(2),
beta=1-tau/m, c=lambda(1+tau/m), a=beta/c, H_0=(), and
H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), j<m. Every child uses these same
enclosing parameters. Write h=H_m, n=2^m-1, and D=n+1.

The tree is the full rooted b-ary tree of depth D with b=64.
Each edge is oriented from parent to child. Put

    kappa=65+16*cos(pi/(D+1)),
    delta_l=kappa*b^(-l)/h_(l-1), 1<=l<=n,
    delta_D=b^(-n)/2.

Every edge has weight 1/kappa. For delta>0 define

    psi_delta(s)=0                         if s<=0,
                 s^2/2                    if 0<=s<=delta,
                 delta*s-delta^2/2         if s>=delta.

On the full vertex Euclidean space, the static objective is

    F(x)=(1/kappa)*sum_(parent p -> child v)
                         psi_(delta_depth(v))(x_p-x_v).

The initial vector has root coordinate1 and every other coordinate0.
The declared physical smoothness is L=1. No function, cap, weight,
state or physical step is changed during a run. The terminal depth-D
edges are present from the outset; no extra physical step is appended.

The intended mechanism is exact forward transfer to all children,
with previous incoming edges inactive. The joint proof must account
for all vertex multiplicities, the sharp full-space smoothness and
the true distance to the full minimum cone. The expected obstruction
is dilution of the conserved positive mass. This is not the centered
leaf simplex, the arithmetic rank body, or a directed-cycle energy.

All new files belong to this public directory. No mathematical
program, numerical evaluation, recurrence execution, solver,
benchmark DP, formal submission, installation, consult or subagent
is allocated. No further profile or round is authorized by this file.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001478 -->
# max2 round58 handoff: exact nonlinear tree law, scoped failure

The allocated analytical round has a complete JOINT UNVERIFIED draft,
tree_pulse_joint_proof.md. There is no new accepted fact and no formal
submission. The original all-word minimax problem remains OPEN.

The frozen construction is one outward64-ary tree of depth
D=n+1=2^m on the actual H_(m,1/4), m>=2. Its one-sided Huber caps
are fixed by depth before the run. Every original physical step moves
the current generation's entire positive pulse into its64 children.

The full proof derives:

- Exact full-space incidence norm squared
  kappa=65+16*cos(pi/(D+1)); the function's least smoothness is1.
- The radial quotient metric sum_l 64^l*y_l^2 and the actual gradient,
  including one incoming and64 outgoing edge contributions.
- Full minimum cone parent<=child, nearest initial point(1/N)*1,
  and true initial R^2=1-1/N, N=(64^(n+2)-1)/63.
- Exact query k state64^(-k) on every vertex of level k and0 elsewhere,
  for ALL k=0,...,n. Every step changes the saturated generation.
- Terminal V_m=3*64^(1-n)/[4*kappa*(1-1/N)], and
  64^n*V_m ->16/27. There are no omitted old-edge values.
- Same-word V_m/Huber ->0 and finite ORIGINAL all-split bound
  B_n*V_m<(3/2)*16^(-n) for every m>=2, with B_n<=4^n proved over
  all original splits inside the joint draft.
- Actual adjacent gradient inner products are negative. At m>=10,
  the largest physical step strictly increases the objective.

The obstruction is pure outward positive-mass dilution, not a missing
suffix estimate. Under depth symmetry, mass conservation alone forces
amplitude64^(-k) for any exact one-generation-at-a-time transfer.
Smoothness then bounds the whole pure-transfer template's payoff by
64^(-n)/(1-1/N). Retuning caps while retaining that template cannot
restore benchmark-scale error.

This does not exclude retained old mass, overlapping generations,
signed states, different starts, within-level asymmetry, inward or
two-sided energies, or other trees. No second profile was selected,
and no general graph/probability impossibility or W upper bound is
claimed. SOURCE_MAP.md states each source's actual hypothesis scope.

Resource handoff: mathematical programs0, numerical evaluations0,
arithmetic/trajectory/recurrence replays0, solvers0, benchmark DP0,
formal/fact_submit0, installations0, consults0, subagents0, repairs0.
Only textual reasoning, retrieval and administrative file operations
were used. No global truth store was hand edited.

Completed baseline57; this is the single allocation through completed
ceiling58/attempted ceiling59. The post-return outer-loop counter is
not observed here. Latest steering read:970c491889a34251, preserving
max2's analytical tree task. No renewal or new formal allocation is
inferred. No publication, finalization, revocation or shutdown occurred.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001479 -->
# Sources and hypothesis boundaries

All new mathematics is in the single UNVERIFIED joint proof
tree_pulse_joint_proof.md. Its elementary sublemmas are parts of that
draft, not accepted inputs. No external mathematical result is used.

| Source | Role and scope actually used |
| --- | --- |
| Accepted 04219554e41d9750 | Original all-split C_n,B_n definitions; optional positive finite dyadic-ray limit in equation(33). Its conditional seed hypothesis is not asserted. The coarse bound B_n<=4^n is proved in this draft for every original split. |
| Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 | Normalization and interpolation convention only. All interpolation inequalities needed for the actual tree samples are proved directly in Section7. No terminal-gradient elimination is used. |
| Accepted 0e7e78056dc6258c | Scope check only. Its expected-orthogonality hypothesis fails by the negative adjacent inner products in equation(24); it is not a premise. |
| Accepted 5fd396eba75651e7 | Awareness only: its two spiral-family obstruction does not upper-bound this tree family or unrestricted W. |
| Finding 37ec6ea9a52bfbdf | Cycle suffix remains open; midpoint retention is insufficient. No premise imported. |
| Finding 1772d8cd97cb1b3e | Coherent sources and late-source prices remain unresolved; diagonal squared-source payment is invalid there. No such payment made here. |
| Finding 037f4a01133b38bc | Finite cycle numerical observations only. No experimental data used in a proof. |
| Finding 5bd29f9ba64b5f1b | Scaled centered leaf-simplex projection pilot, with open all-depth suffix. This is not the present vertex-edge objective. |
| Finding 34bf5f19be16ad26 | Rank-body extinct-mode radius surcharge. This draft instead starts with one root pulse and proves loss by conserved-mass dilution. |

The primary paper retrieved was Abderrahim Elmoataz, Olivier Lezoray
and Sebastien Bougleux, *Nonlocal Discrete Regularization on Weighted
Graphs: a Framework for Image and Manifold Processing* (2008),
[author-hosted PDF](https://lezoray.users.greyc.fr/Publis/IEEE_IP_2008.pdf).
The downloaded PDF is downloads/elmoataz_lezoray_bougleux_2008.pdf.
Web-extracted definitions, incident-edge differentiation and the
Proposition5 proof were read. Equation20 is a normalized Gauss--Jacobi
update; its nonnegative averaging coefficients are not the prescribed
long-step GD coefficients. The model is undirected. Neither its
positivity nor its convergence statement was applied here. The useful
idea was to derive each flux from its energy in the actual vertex metric.

Matlas searches covered clipped tree diffusion and the full regular-tree
Laplacian norm. Returned statements concerned different operators or
boundary problems; none matched the needed claims. The incidence norm
is proved directly through level norms and a finite sine eigenbasis.
The PDF extraction packages pypdf, fitz and pdfminer were absent.
No installation was attempted; the web tool supplied the primary-PDF
text. No arXiv theorem or paper identifier is fabricated.

The selected tree is directed only in its one-sided edge ENERGY. The
Euclidean space still includes every vertex. Its minimum is the full
order cone, and its least global gradient Lipschitz constant is exactly1.
The depth-weighted quotient and full-space spectral norm are different
calculations; both are included. The exact positive pulse law is proved
on the trajectory and is not inferred from a global Markov theorem.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-001480 -->
# An outward Huber tree: exact nonlinear transport and terminal dilution

JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL DRAFT. Worker max2, allocated round58,
2026-10-01. The sole frozen mechanism is in FREEZE.md and shared plan
05c8afbb7b4b4d44. No formal gate is authorized, so none of the new
statements below is a fact. Every new mathematical step is proved in
this one joint argument. No numerical or symbolic program was run.

The construction gives an actual static tree-edge objective and an exact
nonlinear trajectory on every original step of H_(m,1/4). It fails as a
competitive lower witness: its terminal payoff is asymptotic to
(16/27)*64^(-(2^m-1)), and its ratio to the ORIGINAL all-split benchmark
tends to zero. This is an obstruction to this positive, pure forward
transport mechanism, not to every tree energy or to unrestricted W.

## 1. Definitions, fixed instance, and the proposed result

Fix an integer m>=2 and tau=1/4. Define

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), beta=1-tau/m,
    c=lambda*(1+tau/m), a=beta/c,
    t_j=1+a*c^j, 0<=j<m,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,t_j,H_j), 0<=j<m,
    h=H_m=(h_0,...,h_(n-1)), n=2^m-1, D=n+1.             (1)

Every child in (1) uses the SAME enclosing (m,tau). In particular,
h_k=t_(nu_2(k+1)), where nu_2(r) is the largest nonnegative integer j
such that 2^j divides the positive integer r. This identity follows
inductively from the concatenation: adding 2^j to an integer strictly
between 0 and 2^j preserves its smaller binary valuation. No recurrence
in (1), or any trajectory below, was executed by a program.

Let b=64. The vertices of T_D are all words of length at most D over
an alphabet with b letters. The root r is the empty word, depth(v) is
word length, and a parent p is connected to each word obtained by
appending one letter. Orient each edge p->v away from the root.
There are b^l vertices and b^l incoming edges at depth l>=1, and

    N=sum_(l=0)^D b^l=(b^(D+1)-1)/(b-1).                 (2)

The ambient inner product is the ordinary sum over ALL N vertices.
Define the positive constant and caps

    kappa=b+1+2*sqrt(b)*cos(pi/(D+1))
         =65+16*cos(pi/(D+1)),
    delta_l=kappa*b^(-l)/h_(l-1), 1<=l<=n,
    delta_D=b^(-n)/2.                                   (3)

For a positive real delta, set

    psi_delta(s)=0,                         s<=0,
                 s^2/2,                    0<=s<=delta,
                 delta*s-delta^2/2,         s>=delta,
    q_delta(s)=min(delta,max(s,0)).                       (4)

The formulas match at the endpoints, including their first derivatives.
This is a one-sided Huber energy: it is C^1 with Lipschitz derivative,
as required by smooth convex optimization; C^infinity is not asserted.
The minimum cone allowed in TASK.md is relevant to this one-sided choice.

Define one static function and start by

    F(x)=(1/kappa)*sum_(p->v) psi_(delta_depth(v))(x_p-x_v),
    x_0=e_r,
    x_(k+1)=x_k-h_k*grad F(x_k), 0<=k<n.                (5)

Here e_r has value1 at the root and0 at every other vertex. All edges,
including the depth-D edges used in the terminal value, are present
before the first query. Caps depend only on depth and the frozen word.
There is no checkpoint selection, time compression, state reset, new
edge insertion or appended step. The horizon-dependent finite dimension
is enormous but legal; neither the tree nor its vectors were enumerated.

The joint conclusions to be proved are as follows. The least global
gradient Lipschitz constant of F is exactly1, its minimum value is0,
and its full minimum set and true initial distance are

    K={z: z_p<=z_v for every edge p->v},
    x_star=(1/N)*1,
    R^2=dist(e_r,K)^2=1-1/N.                            (6)

The symbol 1 is the all-ones vertex vector. At EVERY physical query,
including the unqueried terminal point, the actual state is

    x_k(v)=b^(-k) if depth(v)=k, and0 otherwise,
                        0<=k<=n.                       (7)

Thus one whole outgoing generation is saturated at each query; the
previous incoming generation is strictly inactive. There are n actual
nonlinear handoffs, with n tending to infinity, rather than one local
activation followed by a fixed quadratic product.

The exact normalized terminal objective, using its closest minimizer, is

    V_m=2*F(x_n)/R^2
       =3*b^(1-n)/[4*kappa*(1-1/N)].                    (8)

For every L>0 the static function f=L*F has least smoothness L;
the physical updates h_k/L give the same states and the same payoff.

Write S=sum_(k=0)^(n-1) h_k and H_base=1/(1+2S). The original benchmark
definitions are those of accepted fact04219554e41d9750:

    C_0=B_0=1,
    Omega(u,v)=(u+v+sqrt((u+v)^2+4uv))/2,
    G(u,v)=2u+v/2+sqrt(v^2+8uv)/2,
    C_j=max_(0<=i<j) Omega(C_i,C_(j-i-1)),
    B_j=max_(0<=i<j) G(C_i,B_(j-i-1)), U_j^ZJ=1/B_j.  (9)

Every original split occurs in (9). We will prove the finite bounds

    0<V_m/H_base<(9/2)*6^m*b^(1-2^m)<1,
    0<B_n*V_m<(3/2)*16^(-n)<1,       m>=2.            (10)

Both displayed upper bounds tend to zero. The sharp asymptotic within
this frozen family is

    b^n*V_m ->16/27.                                   (11)

These bounds concern the single witness (5). Its legality implies
W_n(H_m)>=V_m; an upper bound on V_m gives no upper bound on W_n.

## 2. Smoothness in the full vertex space, including all overlaps

Let A be the oriented incidence map, (Ax)_(p->v)=x_p-x_v. Each
q_delta in (4) is nondecreasing and 1-Lipschitz. Thus F is everywhere
finite, convex, continuously differentiable, and

    grad F(x)=(1/kappa)*A^T q(Ax),                       (12)

where the edge coordinate of q uses that edge's fixed cap. It remains
to compute the FULL-space norm of A, not the norm of a convenient
subspace or a disjoint-edge approximation.

For any vertex vector x put

    U_l=(sum_(depth(v)=l) x_v^2)^(1/2), 0<=l<=D.

At depth l, each parent coordinate is repeated b times. The
Cauchy--Schwarz inequality gives

    abs(sum_(edges p->v at depth l) x_p*x_v)
       <=sqrt(b)*U_(l-1)*U_l.

Consequently

    ||Ax||^2 <=sum_(l=1)^D (sqrt(b)*U_(l-1)+U_l)^2
              =||J U||^2,                              (13)

where J is the D by (D+1) matrix with sqrt(b) in position (l,l-1),
1 in position (l,l), and zero elsewhere, with rows1,...,D.
The symmetric matrix J J^T has diagonal b+1 and its two neighboring
diagonals equal sqrt(b). For r=1,...,D, the vector

    v_l^(r)=sin(l*r*pi/(D+1)), l=1,...,D,

is nonzero, has zero boundary values at l=0,D+1, and satisfies

    (J J^T)v^(r)
       =[b+1+2*sqrt(b)*cos(r*pi/(D+1))]*v^(r).           (14)

This follows by adding the sine values at l-1 and l+1. The D
eigenvalues are distinct, so the corresponding eigenvectors are
linearly independent and exhaust this D-dimensional symmetric matrix.
Their largest eigenvalue is kappa. Thus (13) proves ||A||^2<=kappa.

For equality, take the positive vector v_l=sin(l*pi/(D+1)) and let
U=J^T v. All its entries are positive. Equation (14) gives
J^T J U=kappa U. Set the actual vertex coordinates to

    x_v=(-1)^l*b^(-l/2)*U_l, depth(v)=l.

All adjacent coordinates have opposite signs, and within each level
their magnitudes are equal. Equality holds in every bound in (13).
This vector therefore has ||Ax||^2=kappa||x||^2, proving

    ||A||^2=kappa.                                    (15)

By (12) and the coordinatewise Lipschitz bound,

    ||grad F(x)-grad F(y)||
       <=||A||^2*||x-y||/kappa=||x-y||.

The constant1 is sharp. Choose a level-constant vector u with
u_0=0 and u_l=u_(l-1)-delta_l/2. Every edge difference is strictly
between0 and its cap. There is an open neighborhood of u where
F is quadratic with Hessian A^T A/kappa. By (15) this Hessian has
norm1; a sufficiently small displacement along its top eigenvector
stays in that neighborhood and attains gradient difference ratio1.
Every cap is positive and the tree is finite, so such a neighborhood
exists even though its radius can be very small. No smoothness
discount or coefficient-space norm is used in the physical updates.

## 3. Symmetry reduction with the correct Euclidean metric

Permuting the b children at any vertex leaves the entire static
objective and the initial vector invariant. Equation (12) shows that
its gradient commutes with these permutations. Induction on the
physical steps therefore makes every x_k constant on each depth.

For a general level-constant state let y_l denote the value at depth l.
The induced inner product and energy are

    <y,z>_tree=sum_(l=0)^D b^l*y_l*z_l,
    F(y)=(1/kappa)*sum_(l=1)^D b^l*
                         psi_(delta_l)(y_(l-1)-y_l).    (16)

Put r_l=q_(delta_l)(y_(l-1)-y_l) for1<=l<=D and set r_0=r_(D+1)=0.
At a vertex of depth l the exact full-space gradient is

    g_l=(b*r_(l+1)-r_l)/kappa, 0<=l<=D.               (17)

For the root there are b outgoing edges and no incoming edge; for
a leaf there is one incoming edge and no outgoing edge. At an interior
vertex there is exactly one incoming edge and b outgoing edges. This
proves (17) directly. Equivalently, differentiating (16) and dividing
the l-th derivative by the metric weight b^l gives (17).

Hence the physical reduced updates are

    y_l^+=y_l-(h/kappa)*(b*r_(l+1)-r_l).               (18)

Using the ordinary unweighted gradient of (16) would insert a false
factor b^l. The genuinely Euclidean reduced coordinates are
z_l=b^(l/2)*y_l. In these coordinates the claimed pulse has just
z_k=b^(-k/2) nonzero. This already indicates why an exponentially
growing number of children does not preserve the squared norm.

Summing the full vertex gradient in (12) gives0, because every
incidence row sums to0. Every actual GD update, of any step length,
therefore preserves the sum of the vertex coordinates. This is a
gradient identity; no stochastic-kernel comparison is invoked.

## 4. The full minimum cone and the closest initial point

Every term in F is nonnegative, and psi_delta(s)=0 holds exactly
when s<=0. Its minimum set is therefore exactly K in (6), not
merely the line of constants. Constants belong to K, and F has
minimum0. On a rooted tree, every z in K has z_r<=z_v for every
vertex v, by following its path from the root.

Let p=(1/N)*1. For any z in K,

    <e_r-p,z-p>=z_r-(1/N)*sum_v z_v<=0.                (19)

The constant terms cancel because the coordinates of e_r-p sum to0.
The identity

    ||e_r-z||^2=||e_r-p||^2+||z-p||^2
                         -2<e_r-p,z-p>

then proves that p is a nearest point of the ENTIRE cone K. It is
the unique nearest point, since equality with z!=p is prevented
by the strictly positive ||z-p||^2. Direct expansion gives

    ||e_r-p||^2=(1-1/N)^2+(N-1)/N^2=1-1/N.

In particular R is positive, and the convenient minimizer0 would
give a different, larger radius. The denominator in (8) uses the
actual value in (6). Every vertex, including all late descendants,
has been counted in N and in this projection.

## 5. Every physical step gives a strict saturated handoff

First check that the cap ratios needed for (7) really are below1
on the ORIGINAL hierarchy. Since m>=2 and tau=1/4,

    beta>=7/8, c<=9*lambda/8<3,
    h_k>=t_0=1+beta/c>31/24>81/64.

The last rational comparison follows from 31*64>81*24. Since
D>=4, formula (3) gives b<kappa<81. Thus

    0<kappa/(b*h_k)<1,           0<=k<n.              (20)

Now prove (7) by induction on the ACTUAL physical index k. It is
the prescribed start at k=0. Suppose it holds at k<n and write
A_k=b^(-k). At that state the outgoing depth-(k+1) edges have
difference A_k. Their fixed cap satisfies

    delta_(k+1)/A_k=kappa/(b*h_k)<1.

They are therefore strictly saturated, all with clipped flux
delta_(k+1). If k>=1, every incoming depth-k edge has difference
-A_k and contributes zero flux and zero energy. Every other edge
has zero difference and contributes zero flux and zero energy.

Using (17), the full gradient has the exact coordinates

    grad F(x_k)(v)= A_k/h_k,       depth(v)=k,
                    -A_k/(b*h_k), depth(v)=k+1,
                    0,            all other depths.   (21)

Subtracting h_k times (21) makes every depth-k coordinate zero
and every depth-(k+1) coordinate A_k/b=b^(-(k+1)). It leaves
all other coordinates unchanged. This proves the induction through
k=n, without dropping any small or large physical step.

At the terminal query the only positive edge differences are those
at depth D=n+1, equal to A_n=b^(-n). They are strictly saturated
since their cap is A_n/2. Thus the final value uses the actual
static function, including all old and future edges already present.

This is a growing nonlinear evolution, not a quadratic sector.
At each segment between successive iterates, parametrized by
x(s)=(1-s)x_k+s*x_(k+1), 0<=s<=1, an outgoing depth-(k+1)
difference changes as

    A_k*[1-(1+1/b)*s].                                (22)

It starts above its cap, crosses the cap strictly before its zero
at s=b/(b+1), and ends negative. The depth-(k+2) differences are
s*A_k/b; for k<n-1 they cross their fixed positive cap at
s=kappa/(b*h_(k+1)), which lies in (0,1) by (20). For k=n-1,
the corresponding depth-D differences cross their terminal cap at
s=1/2. Consequently new saturated generations replace old ones at
EVERY physical step, with genuine quadratic-to-linear and
quadratic-to-inactive transitions along these segments. No added
queries along the segment are part of the algorithm.

All states in (7) are nonnegative and have coordinate sum1.
These properties are conclusions of the exact update, despite
unbounded physical step sizes. No claim that (18) is a globally
positive Markov operator is needed or made.

## 6. Exact query values, overlap, and an actual energy increase

Counting all b^(k+1) saturated outgoing edges gives, for k<n,

    F(x_k)=b^(-k)*[1/h_k-kappa/(2*b*h_k^2)],
    ||x_k||^2=b^(-k),
    ||grad F(x_k)||^2=(1+1/b)*b^(-k)/h_k^2.           (23)

The value follows by substituting delta_(k+1) from (3) in
b^(k+1)*[delta_(k+1)*A_k-delta_(k+1)^2/2]/kappa.
All other edge values are exactly zero, rather than omitted errors.

Branches are not assumed independent. Consecutive gradients overlap
on their common generation. Formula (21) gives

    <grad F(x_k),grad F(x_(k+1))>
       =-b^(-k-1)/(h_k*h_(k+1))<0,
                                     0<=k<n-1.       (24)

Thus the expected-orthogonality hypothesis of accepted
0e7e78056dc6258c is not satisfied, even deterministically. It is
not an input to the payoff bound. Nonadjacent queried gradients
with index difference at least2 have disjoint supports, but this
does not justify deleting the negative adjacent cross terms.

The trajectory can have increasing objective at a long step.
For a positive s>kappa/b define

    w(s)=1/s-kappa/(2*b*s^2),
    1/(2s)<w(s)<1/s.                                 (25)

At the largest bridge let k_c=2^(m-1)-1, d=t_(m-1).
The next physical step is t_0. By (23)--(25),

    F(x_(k_c+1))/F(x_(k_c))
       =(1/b)*w(t_0)/w(d)>d/(2*b*t_0).                (26)

For m>=10, beta>=7/8, c>2 and t_0<1+1/lambda<3/2 imply

    d> (7/8)*2^(m-2) >= (7/8)*2^8=7*32>3*64
      >2*b*t_0.

Hence the ACTUAL largest bridge strictly increases F whenever
m>=10. The terminal argument below uses the exact values and
does not rely on energy descent, continuous-time diffusion, or
stable positive coefficients for all possible states.

## 7. Terminal payoff and all original interpolation rows

At x_n each of the b^(n+1) depth-D edges contributes

    psi_(A_n/2)(A_n)=A_n^2/2-A_n^2/8=3*A_n^2/8.

Every other edge has zero energy. Therefore

    F(x_n)=3*b^(1-n)/(8*kappa),                        (27)

which with (6) proves (8). The terminal gradient has value
b*A_n/(2*kappa) on depth n, value -A_n/(2*kappa) on depth n+1,
and0 elsewhere. In particular it has not been declared zero or
deleted just because it is unqueried.

For completeness, legality supplies every directed interpolation
row, with no support restriction. Here is a direct proof. For any
convex differentiable 1-smooth function J, integration of its
Lipschitz gradient along a line segment gives

    J(u+v)<=J(u)+<grad J(u),v>+||v||^2/2.

Fix x and put J_x(z)=F(z)-<grad F(x),z>. Convexity makes x a
global minimizer of J_x. Apply the preceding inequality at y with
v=-grad J_x(y), then compare its stepped value with J_x(x). This
gives

    F(y)-F(x)-<grad F(x),y-x>
                         >=||grad F(y)-grad F(x)||^2/2.

Equivalently, for EVERY ordered pair of actual samples i,j,
including i or j equal to n or the closest minimizer star,

    Q_ij=F(x_i)-F(x_j)+<grad F(x_i),x_j-x_i>
                     +||grad F(x_i)-grad F(x_j)||^2/2<=0. (28)

The optimum sample has x_star from (6), F(x_star)=0 and
grad F(x_star)=0. The full Gram matrices of all actual vectors
are positive semidefinite since a quadratic form in such a Gram
matrix is the squared norm of the corresponding vector combination.
This verifies legality directly in the same normalization as
accepted fact1252b8823bb5a290; no terminal-reduction claim is used.

## 8. Same-word Huber and original all-split comparisons

The scalar two-sided Huber function with cap q=1/(1+2S),

    J_q(z)=z^2/2                 if |z|<=q,
           q*|z|-q^2/2          if |z|>=q,

has least smoothness1 and unique minimizer0. From scalar start1,
at every query it has state 1-q*P, where P is the actually executed
prefix sum, because

    1-q*S=(1+S)/(1+2S)>q.

Every step therefore uses gradient q; no intermediate overshoot
is skipped. Its exact normalized terminal value is

    2*q-(2S+1)*q^2=1/(1+2S)=H_base.                 (29)

To compare (8) with (29), use kappa>b and R^2>1/2 to get

    V_m<(3/2)*b^(-n).                                (30)

The largest hierarchy step satisfies, for m>=2,

    t_(m-1)=1+beta*c^(m-2)<c^m<3^m.

Indeed c>lambda>2 and beta<1 imply c^(m-2)*(c^2-beta)>1.
All n steps are bounded by that largest step, so S<6^m and
1+2S<3*6^m. Combining with (30) gives the first upper bound in
(10). For m=2 the expression is 162*b^(-3)<1. On increasing
m by one its ratio is 6*b^(-2^m)<1/2. This proves the strict
finite comparison and convergence to0 without numerical evaluation.

For the ORIGINAL benchmark (9), a deliberately coarse envelope is
sufficient and will be proved instead of replacing the all-split
maximum by a balanced tree. Both Omega and G are nondecreasing
in each nonnegative argument, directly from their formulas. We
claim C_j<=4^j and B_j<=4^j for every nonnegative integer j.
This holds at j=0. If it holds at all indices below j and
0<=i<j, both arguments of either recurrence are at most
M=4^(j-1). Therefore

    Omega(C_i,C_(j-i-1))<=Omega(M,M)=lambda*M<4*M,
    G(C_i,B_(j-i-1))<=G(M,M)=4*M.

Taking the original maxima proves the induction, and in particular

    B_n<=4^n.                                       (31)

Together with (30) and b=64 this gives

    B_n*V_m<(3/2)*(4/b)^n=(3/2)*16^(-n),             (32)

which proves the second part of (10). This is an explicit finite
all-depth bound for the chosen actual instance, not merely a
limiting comparison or an inference from finite experiments.

As m tends to infinity, D tends to infinity, kappa tends to81,
and N tends to infinity. Formula (8) gives

    b^n*V_m ->3*b/(4*81)=16/27,

proving (11). If the precise benchmark scale is wanted, the
unconditional ray conclusion of accepted04219554e41d9750 states
B_(2^m-1)/lambda^m -> Z_1, with 0<Z_1<infinity. Hence

    B_(2^m-1)*V_m
       /[lambda^m*b^(-(2^m-1))] ->(16/27)*Z_1.       (33)

Only that accepted unconditional conclusion is used in (33).
The much simpler finite bound (32) requires only the definitions
in (9) and their induction. None of the accepted fact's conditional
primitive-seed hypotheses is asserted.

## 9. Why retuning caps within pure forward transport cannot repair it

This section describes a conditional obstruction to the SAME
transport template; it selects no second profile or run. Consider
any outward-tree sum of positive-weight one-sided Huber energies
on this same full b-ary tree, with weights and caps constant within
each depth, normalized to gradient Lipschitz constant at most1.
Start at e_r. Suppose its actual trajectory, on an arbitrary
schedule, places all nonzero coordinates entirely in generation k
at query k, for k=0,...,n. The depth symmetry makes the coordinates
equal within that generation. Every edge gradient has zero total
coordinate sum, so mass conservation forces its value to be b^(-k).
It follows necessarily that ||x_n||^2=b^(-n).

Because all weights and caps are positive, its full minimum set is
the SAME cone K and its initial radius is again R^2=1-1/N by (19).
Also0 is a minimizer with gradient0. Applying the line-segment
smoothness inequality at0 gives

    2*F(x_n)<=||x_n||^2=b^(-n),
    2*F(x_n)/R^2<=b^(-n)/(1-1/N)<2*b^(-n).          (34)

This conditional bound already prevents this generation-by-
generation pure transport template from providing benchmark-scale
error on (1), since multiplying it by (31) tends to0. Its hypotheses
are realized by the frozen instance, with the sharper exact (8).

The paid loss is conservation and dilution of a positive pulse.
There are b^n active terminal vertices, but their individual
amplitudes are b^(-n); their squared contributions add to b^(-n).
The true initial radius stays close to1 and cannot cancel this loss.
This is different from the rank-body profile's cost of initially
present modes that later disappear. Here the initial mass is only
at the root, and future generations are generated by actual
overlapping gradients, whose adjacent cross terms are negative.

The obstruction does NOT cover retained mass on old generations,
several simultaneous pulses, signed states, different initial
profiles, asymmetric within-level weights, inward or two-sided
Huber energies, branch recombination, or other trees. In particular
it supplies no assertion that all graph, probability or branching
methods fail. Those possibilities are not authorized new profiles
or a continuation allocation for this round.

## 10. Prior scopes, source hypotheses, and correctness status

The required prior scopes were read before this profile was fixed:

* 37ec6ea9a52bfbdf: the directed-cycle draft has an open terminal
  suffix-energy estimate; a midpoint reversal does not settle it.
* 1772d8cd97cb1b3e: signed cycle kernels retain an unresolved coherent
  source term, and an actual two-step example refutes payment by
  diagonal squared source costs. No such payment occurs here.
* 037f4a01133b38bc: finite cycle observations, including energy
  bursts, are numerical awareness and no all-depth theorem.
* 5bd29f9ba64b5f1b: the scaled centered leaf-body pilot is a
  projection-envelope construction with finite observations and an
  open full suffix law. The vertex-edge energy (5) is a different
  static function, with a different full minimum set.
* 34bf5f19be16ad26: the rank-body staircase has a positive initial
  radius surcharge from extinct modes. No rank body or extinct
  initial modal decomposition is used in this proof.
* Accepted0e7e78056dc6258c bounds expected-orthogonal gradients only.
  Equation (24) explicitly fails that hypothesis here.

These five unverified findings are awareness, not correctness
premises. The accepted stagewise fact5fd396eba75651e7 concerns only
its two spiral families and is not a tree or unrestricted-W bound.

The search-math-results workflow used program_stage=active_program,
active_program=static nonlinear tree-edge construction,
missing_mechanism=full nonlinear transport with paid overlap and
radius, search_mode=analogy, intent=construction/background.
Matlas queries for clipped tree diffusion and for the regular-tree
Laplacian formula returned no usable matching result; none was cited
as a premise. All of (12)--(15) is proved here.

Primary-source orientation was the downloaded paper by Abderrahim
Elmoataz, Olivier Lezoray and Sebastien Bougleux, *Nonlocal Discrete
Regularization on Weighted Graphs: a Framework for Image and Manifold
Processing* (2008), author-hosted PDF:
https://lezoray.users.greyc.fr/Publis/IEEE_IP_2008.pdf . Its vertex
inner product, incident-edge differentiation and Proposition5 proof
were read in the web-extracted PDF. Equation20 uses a normalized
Gauss--Jacobi update with coefficients summing to one. Its positivity
and convergence reasoning concerns that update and an undirected
regularization model. They do not apply to the prescribed long-step
GD or to the present directed one-sided caps. The transferable idea
was to derive the flux and its metric from the actual energy. No
external mathematical theorem enters this joint proof.

The direct-proving decomposition has a joint draft for all of its
subgoals: exact incidence norm; metric and gradient; full minimum
cone and projection; all nonlinear physical stages; all query and
terminal values; original benchmark comparison; precise obstruction
scope. None is promoted separately or treated as a verified brick.
The positive lower-witness objective is refuted WITHIN the selected
pure-forward mechanism by the exact terminal bound, not by a failed
attempt at proving its trajectory.

allocation; no new fact_id exists. The original all-word minimax
Goal remains OPEN. No formal gate, further round, publication,
finalization, revocation, shutdown or automatic renewal is inferred.

## Notation index

m,tau,lambda,beta,c,a,t_j,H_j,h,n,D: hierarchy and physical length
parameters in (1); the same enclosing m,tau are used everywhere.
b=64: branching, distinct from any benchmark ray index.
T_D,r,depth(v),N: rooted tree, root, depth and full vertex count.
kappa,delta_l,psi_delta,q_delta: exact incidence norm squared,
fixed caps, one-sided Huber energy and clipped derivative.
F,f,L,x_k,e_r: static objective, its L-scaled form, physical
smoothness, actual iterates and the root coordinate vector.
K,x_star,R: full minimum cone, closest initial minimizer and radius.
V_m: actual normalized terminal objective (8).
A: full oriented incidence map; A_k=b^(-k) is pulse amplitude.
U_l,J,v^(r): level norm and auxiliary spectral objects in Section2.
y_l,z_l,r_l,g_l: level values, isometric reduced coordinates,
clipped level flux and per-vertex gradient in Section3.

C_j,B_j,Omega,G,U_j^ZJ,Z_1,W_n,Q_ij: original benchmark,
unrestricted objective and interpolation notation as defined here.

of all notation in this proof.
